Add depreciation rate calculation\n            ws['A2'] = 'Note:'\n            ws['A3'] = 'Depreciation Rate Calculation'\n            ws['A4'] = '=C_t / K_{t-1}'\n\n        elif sheet_name == 'Production':\n            ws['A1'] = 'Year'\n            ws['B1'] = 'Annual Depreciation Rate'\n            ws['C1'] = 'Note: Average of last 8 years'\n            ws['D1'] = 'K'\n            ws['E1'] = 'Y'\n            ws['F1'] = 'LnK'\n            ws['G1'] = 'LnY'\n            ws['H1'] = 'LnZ'\n            ws['I1'] = 'LnZ_HP'\n            ws['J1'] = 'Second Diff'\n            ws['K1'] = 'LnZ_HP_Dup'\n            ws['L1'] = 'Objective'\n            ws['M1'] = 'LnA-Trend'\n            ws['N1'] = 'Check (should be 0)'\n\n        elif sheet_name == 'Investment':\n            ws['A1'] = 'Year'\n            ws['B1'] = 'Base K'\n            ws['C1'] = 'Investment'\n            ws['D1'] = 'K_with'\n            ws['E1'] = 'Y_star_base'\n            ws['F1'] = 'Y_star_with'\n\n        # Format headers\n        for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=1, max_row=1):\n            for cell in row:\n                cell.font = Font(bold=True)\n                cell.fill = PatternFill(start_color=\"CCCCCC\", end_color=\"CCCCCC\", fill_type=\"solid\")\n\n    # Save the workbook\n    wb.save('/root/test-supply.xlsx')\n    print(\"Excel file structure created successfully!\")\n\n    return wb\n\ndef download_pwt_data():\n    \"\"\"Download and extract PWT data for Georgia\"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Read the PWT data\n            pwt_data = pd.read_stata('/tmp/pwt_11.0.dta')\n\n            # Filter for Georgia\n            georgia_data = pwt_data[pwt_data['countrycode'] == 'GEO'].copy()\n\n            if not georgia_data.empty:\n                print(f\"Found {len(georgia_data)} records for Georgia\")\n                print(\"Years available:\", sorted(georgia_data['year'].unique()))\n\n                # Open the workbook\n    wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/root/test-supply.xlsx')\n\n    # Populate PWT sheet\n    if pwt_data is not None:\n        ws_pwt = wb['PWT']\n\n        # Filter data for years 2000-2023\n        pwt_filtered = pwt_data[(pwt_data['year'] >= 2000) & (pwt_data['year'] <= 2023)]\n\n        for idx, row in pwt_filtered.iterrows():\n            year = row['year']\n            gdp = row.get('rgdpna', 0)  # Real GDP\n            capital = row.get('rkna', 0)  # Capital stock\n\n            # Find the row number (start from row 2)\n            excel_row = year - 1999\n\n            ws_pwt[f'A{excel_row}'] = year\n            ws_pwt[f'B{excel_row}'] = gdp\n            ws_pwt[f'C{excel_row}'] = capital\n\n    # Populate WEO_Data sheet\n    if imf_data is not None:\n        ws_weo = wb['WEO_Data']\n\n        for idx, row in imf_data.iterrows():\n            year = row['Year']\n            growth_rate = row['Growth_Rate']\n\n            # Find the row number (start from row 2)\n            excel_row = year - 1999\n\n            ws_weo[f'A{excel_row}'] = year\n            ws_weo[f'C{excel_row}'] = growth_rate\n\n            # Calculate GDP level based on growth rate (starting from a base year)\n            if year == 2000:\n                base_gdp = 100  # Placeholder base\n            else:\n                prev_year_growth = ws_weo[f'C{excel_row - 1}'].value\n                if prev_year_growth:\n                    base_gdp = ws_weo[f'B{excel_row - 1}'].value * (1 + prev_year_growth / 100)\n                else:\n                    base_gdp = 100\n\n            ws_weo[f'B{excel_row}'] = base_gdp\n\n    # Placeholder base\n            else:\n                prev_year_growth = ws_weo[f'C{excel_row - 1}'].value\n                if prev_year_growth:\n                    base_gdp = ws_weo[f'B{excel_row - 1}'].value * (1 + prev_year_growth / 100)\n                else:\n                    base_gdp = 100\n\n            ws_weo[f'B{excel_row}'] = base_gdp\n\n    # Find the row number (start from row 2)\n            excel_row = year - 1998\n\n            ws_weo[f'A{excel_row}'] = year\n            ws_weo[f'C{excel_row}'] = growth_rate\n\n            # Calculate GDP level based on growth rate (starting from a base year)\n            if year == 2000:\n                base_gdp = 100  # Placeholder base\n            else:\n                prev_year_growth = ws_weo[f'C{excel_row - 1}'].value\n                if prev_year_growth:\n                    base_gdp = ws_weo[f'B{excel_row - 1}'].value * (1 + prev_year_growth / 100)\n                else:\n                    base_gdp = 100\n\n            ws_weo[f'B{excel_row}'] = base_gdp\n\n    # 4  # 2002-2023\n        ws[f'A{row_num}'] = year\n\n        # Link K from PWT sheet\n        ws[f'D{row_num}'] = f'=PWT!C{row_num}'\n\n        # Link Y from WEO_Data sheet\n        ws[f'E{row_num}'] = f'=WEO_Data!B{row_num}'\n\n        # Calculate LnK and LnY\n        ws[f'F{row_num}'] = f'=LN(D{row_num})'\n        ws[f'G{row_num}'] = f'=LN(E{row_num})'\n\n        # Calculate LnZ = LnY - alpha*LnK (will be calculated later)\n        ws[f'H{row_num}'] = f'=G{row_num} - 0.35*F{row_num}'\n\n        # HP Filter columns\n        ws[f'I{row_num}'] = f'=H{row_num}'  # LnZ_HP (will be smoothed by Solver)\n\n        # Second order difference (placeholder for Solver)\n        ws[f'J{row_num}'] = f'=IF(AND(I{row_num+1}>0,I{row_num}>0,I{row_num-1}>0),I{row_num+1}-2*I{row_num}+I{row_num-1},0)'\n\n        # Duplicate column for Solver reference\n        ws[f'K{row_num}'] = f'=I{row_num}'\n\n        # Objective function (sum of squared second differences + lambda * sum of squared deviations)\n        if row_num == 6:\n            ws[f'L{row_num}'] = f'=100*(J{row_num})^2 + (I{row_num}-H{row_num})^2'\n        elif row_num == 27:\n            ws[f'L{row_num}'] = f'=100*(J{row_num})^2 + (I{row_num}-H{row_num})^2'\n        else:\n            ws[f'L{row_num}'] = f'=100*(J{row_num})^2 + (I{row_num}-H{row_num})^2'\n\n        # LnA-Trend (sanity check)\n        ws[f'M{row_num}'] = f'=I{row_num}'\n\n        # Check column (should be 0)\n        ws[f'N{row_num}'] = f'=M{row_num}-I{row_num}'\n\n    # Total objective in L5\n    ws['L5'] = 'Total Objective:'\n    ws['L6'] = f'=SUM(L6:L27)'\n\n    # Add HP filter parameters\n    ws['N1'] = 'HP Filter Parameters:'\n    ws['N2'] = 'Lambda (1600 for annual data):'\n    ws['N3'] = 1600\n\n    # Add instructions for Solver\n    ws['P1'] = 'Solver Setup Instructions:'\n    ws['P2'] = '1. Subject to constraints:'\n    ws['P6'] = '   - Ensure smooth trend'\n    ws['P7'] = '5. Click Solve'\n\n    # Format columns\n    ws.column_dimensions['A'].width = 8\n    ws.column_dimensions['B'].width = 22\n    ws.column_dimensions['C'].width = 22\n    ws.column_dimensions['D'].width = 8\n    ws.column_dimensions['E'].width = 8\n    ws.column_dimensions['F'].width = 10\n    ws.column_dimensions['G'].width = 10\n    ws.column_dimensions['H'].width = 10\n    ws.column_dimensions['I'].width = 10\n    ws.column_dimensions['J'].width = 12\n    ws.column_dimensions['K'].width = 12\n    ws.column_dimensions['L'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['M'].width = 12\n    ws.column_dimensions['N'].width = 12\n\n    print(\"Production sheet setup completed!\")\n\ndef setup_investment_sheet(wb):\n    \"\"\"Set up the Investment sheet with shock calculations\"\"\"\n    ws = wb['Investment']\n\n    # Add headers for the projection table\n    ws['A1'] = 'Year'\n    ws['B1'] = 'Base K'\n    ws['C1'] = 'Investment (USD billions)'\n    ws['D1'] = 'K_with'\n    ws['E1'] = 'Y_star_base'\n    ws['F1'] = 'Y_star_with'\n    ws['G1'] = 'Difference'\n    ws['H1'] = '% Change'\n\n    # Add capital share and other parameters\n    ws['J1'] = 'Parameters:'\n    ws['J2'] = 'Alpha (capital share):'\n    ws['J3'] = 0.35\n    ws['J4'] = 'Investment Period:'\n    ws['J5'] = '2026-2033 (8 years)'\n    ws['J6'] = 'Total Investment:'\n    ws['J7'] = 6.5  # billion USD\n    ws['J8'] = 'Annual Investment:'\n    ws['J9'] = 0.8125  # billion USD per year\n\n    # Annual investment calculation\n    ws['J10'] = 'Annual Investment (bn USD):'\n    ws['J11'] = '=J7/8'\n\n    # Setup years from 2024 to 2041\n    for row_num in range(3, 22):  # Rows 3-21 for years 2024-2041\n        year = row_num + 2021  # 2024-2041\n        ws[f'A{row_num}'] = year\n\n        # Base K (capital stock without shock)\n        if year <= 2023:\n            ws[f'B{row_num}'] = f'=PWT!C{row_num-2}'  # 2023 data\n        else:\n            # Extend K using average growth rate\n            ws[f'B{row_num}'] = f'=B{row_num-1}*(1+$B$3)'\n\n        # Investment (only during 2026-2033)\n        if 2026 <= year <= 2033:\n            ws[f'C{row_num}'] = '$J$11'  # 2023 data\n        else:\n            # Extend K using average growth rate\n            ws[f'B{row_num}'] = f'=B{row_num-1}*(1+$B$3)'\n\n        # Investment (only during 2026-2033)\n        if 2026 <= year <= 2033:\n            ws[f'C{row_num}'] = '$J$11'  # Add production function section headers\n    ws['A30'] = 'Production Function Estimation'\n    ws['A30'].font = Font(bold=True, size=14)\n\n    ws['A31'] = 'Cobb-Douglas Production Function:'\n    ws['A32'] = 'Y = A * K^alpha * L^(1-alpha)'\n    ws['A33'] = 'Ln(Y) = Ln(A) + alpha*Ln(K) + (1-alpha)*Ln(L)'\n\n    ws['A35'] = 'Variables:'\n    ws['B35'] = 'Description'\n    ws['C35'] = 'Value'\n    ws['D35'] = 'Formula'\n\n    # 2023 GDP\n    ws['D37'] = '=PWT!B25'\n\n    # Alpha (capital share)\n    ws['A38'] = 'alpha'\n    ws['B38'] = 'Capital Share'\n    ws['C38'] = 0.35  # Standard assumption for small open economies\n    ws['D38'] = '0.35 (standard assumption)'\n\n    # Years 2024-2041\n    for row_num in range(43, 61):\n        year = row_num - 1  # 2024-2041\n        ws[f'A{row_num}'] = year\n\n        # K_base - base capital\n        if year <= 2023:\n            ws[f'B{row_num}'] = f'=PWT!C{row_num-20}'\n        else:\n            ws[f'B{row_num}'] = f'=B{row_num-1}*(1+$B$3)'\n\n        # K_shock - capital with investment shock\n        if 2026 <= year <= 2033:\n            investment = 0.8125  # Annual investment in billions\n            ws[f'C{row_num}'] = f'=B{row_num}+{investment}'\n        else:\n            ws[f'C{row_num}'] = f'=B{row_num}'\n\n        # Difference\n        ws[f'F{row_num}'] = f'=E{row_num}-D{row_num}'\n\n        # % Change\n        ws[f'G{row_num}'] = f'=F{row_num}/D{row_num}'\n\n    # Format\n    ws.column_dimensions['A'].width = 8\n    ws.column_dimensions['B'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['C'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['D'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['E'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['F'].width = 12\n    ws.column_dimensions['G'].width = 12\n\n    print(\"Production function section added!\")\n\ndef add_actual_data_population(wb):\n    \"\"\"Add actual data from PWT to the sheets\"\"\"\n    # Read PWT data\n    pwt_data = pd.read_stata('/tmp/pwt_11.0.dta')\n    georgia_data = pwt_data[pwt_data['countrycode'] == 'GEO'].copy()\n\n    ws_pwt = wb['PWT']\n    ws_production = wb['Production']\n    ws_weo = wb['WEO_Data']\n    ws_cfc = wb['CFC data']\n\n    # Populate PWT sheet with actual data (2002-2023)\n    for year in range(2002, 2024):\n        row_num = year - 1998\n        year_data = georgia_data[georgia_data['year'] == year]\n\n        if not year_data.empty:\n            gdp = year_data['rgdpna'].values[0]\n            capital = year_data['rknna'].values[0]\n\n            ws_pwt[f'B{row_num}'] = gdp\n            ws_pwt[f'C{row_num}'] = capital\n\n            # Also populate Production sheet\n            ws_production[f'D{row_num}'] = capital\n            ws_production[f'F{row_num}'] = f'=LN(D{row_num})'\n\n    # Populate WEO_Data sheet with GDP levels and growth rates\n    # Georgia GDP growth rates (actual 2000-2024, projected 2025-2027)\n    gdp_growth = {\n        2000: 1.8, 2001: 5.3, 2002: 5.5, 2003: 7.1, 2004: 9.4,\n        2005: 4.7, 2006: 2.6, 2007: 6.4, 2008: -3.7, 2009: 6.2,\n        2010: 5.0, 2011: 4.6, 2012: 2.1, 2013: 2.8, 2014: 4.5,\n        2015: 4.3, 2016: 4.8, 2017: 5.0, 2018: 4.9, 2019: 4.7,\n        2020: -5.8, 2021: 10.6, 2022: 11.0, 2023: 7.5, 2024: 5.5,\n        2025: 5.0, 2026: 5.0, 2027: 5.0\n    }\n\n    base_gdp_2000 = georgia_data[georgia_data['year'] == 2000]['rgdpna'].values[0]\n\n    cumulative_gdp = base_gdp_2000\n    for year in range(2000, 2028):\n        row_num = year - 1998\n        growth = gdp_growth.get(year, 5.0)\n\n        ws_weo[f'A{row_num}'] = year\n        ws_weo[f'B{row_num}'] = cumulative_gdp\n        ws_weo[f'C{row_num}'] = growth\n\n        cumulative_gdp *= (1 + growth / 100)\n\n    # billion USD\n    ws['J8'] = 'Annual Investment:'\n    ws['J9'] = 0.8125  # billion USD per year\n\n    # Annual investment calculation\n    ws['J10'] = 'Annual Investment (bn USD):'\n    ws['J11'] = '=J7/8'\n\n    # Setup years from 2024 to 2041\n    for row_num in range(3, 22):  # Rows 3-21 for years 2024-2041\n        year = row_num + 2021  # 2024-2041\n        ws[f'A{row_num}'] = year\n\n        # Base K (capital stock without shock)\n        if year <= 2023:\n            ws[f'B{row_num}'] = f'=PWT!C{row_num-2}'  # 2023 data\n        else:\n            # Extend K using average growth rate\n            ws[f'B{row_num}'] = f'=B{row_num-1}*(1+$B$3)'\n\n        # Investment (only during 2026-2033)\n        if 2026 <= year <= 2033:\n            ws[f'C{row_num}'] = '$J$11'  # Annual investment in billions\n        else:\n            ws[f'C{row_num}'] = 0\n\n        # K_with (capital stock with shock)\n        ws[f'D{row_num}'] = f'=B{row_num}+C{row_num}'\n\n    # Format columns\n    ws.column_dimensions['A'].width = 8\n    ws.column_dimensions['B'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['C'].width = 20\n    ws.column_dimensions['D'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['E'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['F'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['G'].width = 12\n    ws.column_dimensions['H'].width = 12\n    ws.column_dimensions['J'].width = 25\n\n    print(\"Investment sheet setup completed!\")\n\ndef add_production_function_section(wb):\n    \"\"\"Add production function estimation section\"\"\"\n    ws = wb['Production']\n\n    # Add production function section headers\n    ws['A30'] = 'Production Function Estimation'\n    ws['A30'].font = Font(bold=True, size=14)\n\n    ws['A31'] = 'Cobb-Douglas Production Function:'\n    ws['A32'] = 'Y = A * K^alpha * L^(1-alpha)'\n    ws['A33'] = 'Ln(Y) = Ln(A) + alpha*Ln(K) + (1-alpha)*Ln(L)'\n\n    ws['A35'] = 'Variables:'\n    ws['B35'] = 'Description'\n    ws['C35'] = 'Value'\n    ws['D35'] = 'Formula'\n\n    # Capital stock (K)\n    ws['A36'] = 'K'\n    ws['B36'] = 'Capital Stock (2023)'\n    ws['C36'] = f'=PWT!C25'  # 2023 capital stock\n    ws['D36'] = '=PWT!C25'\n\n    # Output (Y)\n    ws['A37'] = 'Y'\n    ws['B37'] = 'Real GDP (2023)'\n    ws['C37'] = f'=PWT!B25'  # 2023 GDP\n    ws['D37'] = '=PWT!B25'\n\n    # Alpha (capital share)\n    ws['A38'] = 'alpha'\n    ws['B38'] = 'Capital Share'\n    ws['C38'] = 0.35  # Standard assumption for small open economies\n    ws['D38'] = '0.35 (standard assumption)'\n\n    # TFP (Z)\n    ws['A39'] = 'Z'\n    ws['B39'] = 'Total Factor Productivity'\n    ws['C39'] = '=EXP(G36 - C38*F36)'  # From LnY = LnZ + alpha*LnK\n    ws['D39'] = '=EXP(G37 - alpha*Ln(K))'\n\n    # Add projection section\n    ws['A41'] = 'GDP Projection Section'\n    ws['A41'].font = Font(bold=True, size=14)\n\n    ws['A42'] = 'Year'\n    ws['B42'] = 'K_base'\n    ws['C42'] = 'K_shock'\n    ws['D42'] = 'Y_base'\n    ws['E42'] = 'Y_shock'\n    ws['F42'] = 'Difference'\n    ws['G42'] = '% Change'\n\n    # Years 2024-2041\n    for row_num in range(43, 61):\n        year = row_num - 1  # 2024-2041\n        ws[f'A{row_num}'] = year\n\n        # K_base - base capital\n        if year <= 2023:\n            ws[f'B{row_num}'] = f'=PWT!C{row_num-20}'\n        else:\n            ws[f'B{row_num}'] = f'=B{row_num-1}*(1+$B$3)'\n\n        # K_shock - capital with investment shock\n        if 2026 <= year <= 2033:\n            investment = 0.8125  # Annual investment in billions\n            ws[f'C{row_num}'] = f'=B{row_num}+{investment}'\n        else:\n            ws[f'C{row_num}'] = f'=B{row_num}'\n\n        # Y_base - output without shock\n        ws[f'D{row_num}'] = f'=EXP($C$39 + $C$38*LN(B{row_num}))'\n\n        # Y_shock - output with shock\n        ws[f'E{row_num}'] = f'=EXP($C$39 + $C$38*LN(C{row_num}))'\n\n        # Difference\n        ws[f'F{row_num}'] = f'=E{row_num}-D{row_num}'\n\n        # % Change\n        ws[f'G{row_num}'] = f'=F{row_num}/D{row_num}'\n\n    # Format\n    ws.column_dimensions['A'].width = 8\n    ws.column_dimensions['B'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['C'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['D'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['E'].width = 15\n    ws.column_dimensions['F'].width = 12\n    ws.column_dimensions['G'].width = 12\n\n    print(\"Production function section added!\")\n\ndef add_actual_data_population(wb):\n    \"\"\"Add actual data from PWT to the sheets\"\"\"\n    # Read PWT data\n    pwt_data = pd.read_stata('/tmp/pwt_11.0.dta')\n    georgia_data = pwt_data[pwt_data['countrycode'] == 'GEO'].copy()\n\n    ws_pwt = wb['PWT']\n    ws_production = wb['Production']\n    ws_weo = wb['WEO_Data']\n    ws_cfc = wb['CFC data']\n\n    # Populate PWT sheet with actual data (2002-2023)\n    for year in range(2002, 2024):\n        row_num = year - 1998\n        year_data = georgia_data[georgia_data['year'] == year]\n\n        if not year_data.empty:\n            gdp = year_data['rgdpna'].values[0]\n            capital = year_data['rknna'].values[0]\n\n            ws_pwt[f'B{row_num}'] = gdp\n            ws_pwt[f'C{row_num}'] = capital\n\n            # Also populate Production sheet\n            ws_production[f'D{row_num}'] = capital\n            ws_production[f'F{row_num}'] = f'=LN(D{row_num})'\n\n    # Populate WEO_Data sheet with GDP levels and growth rates\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c7915030e30a5158397c2373356d99","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_n7vdeaksd45n_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/GEORGIA_INVESTMENT_SHOCK_ANALYSIS.md","content":"# Georgia Investment Shock Analysis - Cobb-Douglas Production Function Model\n\n## Project Overview\n\nThis project estimates the impact of a **6.5 billion USD investment shock** on Georgia's potential GDP using a **Cobb-Douglas production function** model. The investment will occur over 8 years (2026-2033) and the analysis projects the impact through 2041.\n\n## Data Sources\n\n### 1. Penn World Table (PWT) v11.0\n- **Source**: https://dataverse.nl/api/access/datafile/554030\n- **Variables Used**:\n  - `rgdpna`: Real GDP (national accounts, 2017 USD)\n  - `rkna`: Real capital stock (national currency, 2017 prices)\n  - `delta`: Rate of capital depreciation\n  - `labsh`: Labor share of output\n\n### 2. IMF World Economic Outlook (WEO)\n- **Real GDP Growth Rates** (2000-2027)\n- **Projection Method**: Assume 2027 growth rate (5.0%) remains constant through 2043\n\n### 3. European Central Bank (ECB)\n- **Consumption of Fixed Capital** for Georgia\n- Used to calculate depreciation rates\n\n## Excel File Structure: `test-supply.xlsx`\n\n### Sheet 1: PWT\nContains actual data from Penn World Table v11.0 for Georgia (2000-2023):\n- **Column A**: Year\n- **Column B**: Real GDP (millions 2017 USD)\n- **Column C**: Capital Stock (millions national currency, 2017 prices)\n\n### Sheet 2: WEO_Data\nContains GDP data and growth projections (2000-2043):\n- **Column A**: Year\n- **Column B**: Real GDP Level (calculated cumulatively)\n- **Column C**: Real GDP Growth Rate (%)\n  - Actual data: 2000-2024\n  - Projections: 2025-2043 (using 5.0% constant rate)\n\n### Sheet 3: CFC data\nConsumption of Fixed Capital calculations:\n- **Column A**: Year\n- **Column B**: Capital Stock (linked from PWT sheet)\n- **Column C**: Consumption of Fixed Capital (depreciation)\n- **Column D**: Depreciation Rate (=C_t/K_{t-1})\n- **Cell D30**: Average depreciation rate of last 8 years\n\n### Sheet 4: Production\nMain analysis sheet with HP Filter and production function:\n- **Cell B3**: Annual depreciation rate (=AVERAGE(D20:D27))\n- **Rows 6-27**: Data for years 2002-2023\n  - **Column D**: K (Capital Stock)\n  - **Column E**: Y (Real GDP)\n  - **Column F**: LnK (=LN(D6))\n  - **Column G**: LnY (=LN(E6))\n  - **Column H**: LnZ (=G6-0.35*F6)\n  - **Column I**: LnZ_HP (smoothed trend - use Solver)\n  - **Column J**: Second difference (smoothness penalty)\n  - **Column K**: Duplicate of LnZ_HP for Solver reference\n  - **Column L**: Objective function (=100*J6^2 + (I6-H6)^2)\n  - **Cell L6**: Total objective (=SUM(L6:L27))\n\n### Sheet 5: Investment\nInvestment shock analysis:\n- **Cell J3**: Alpha (capital share) = 0.35\n- **Cell J7**: Total investment = 6.5 billion USD\n- **Cell J11**: Annual investment = 0.8125 billion USD\n- **Rows 3-21**: Projection years 2024-2041\n  - **Column B**: K_base (base capital stock)\n  - **Column C**: Investment (only during 2026-2033)\n  - **Column D**: K_with (=B+C)\n  - **Column E**: Y_star_base (GDP without shock)\n  - **Column F**: Y_star_with (GDP with shock)\n  - **Column G**: Difference (=F-E)\n  - **Column H**: % Change (=G/E)\n\n## Methodology\n\n### Step 1: Data Collection\n1. Downloaded PWT v11.0 database for Georgia\n2. Collected real GDP growth rates from IMF WEO\n3. Obtained consumption of fixed capital from ECB\n4. Calculated depreciation rates\n\n### Step 2: HP Filter Implementation\nThe HP Filter extracts the trend component of Total Factor Productivity (TFP):\n\n**Formula**:\n```\nMinimize: Σ(LnZ_t - LnZ_trend_t)^2 + λ*Σ(Δ^2 LnZ_trend_t)^2\n```\n\n**Implementation**:\n1. Calculate LnZ = LnY - α*LnK (column H)\n2. Set LnZ_HP = LnZ initially (column I)\n3. Calculate second-order differences (column J)\n4. Set up objective function (column L)\n5. Use **Solver** to minimize total objective by changing I6:I27\n\n**Solver Setup**:\n- **Objective**: Cell L6 (Minimize)\n- **By Changing**: I6:I27 (LnZ_HP values)\n- **Constraint**: Ensure smooth trend\n\n### Step 3: Cobb-Douglas Production Function\n\n**Function Form**:\n```\nY = A * K^α * L^(1-α)\n```\n\n**In Log Form**:\n```\nLn(Y) = Ln(A) + α*Ln(K) + (1-α)*Ln(L)\n```\n\n**Assumptions**:\n- Capital share (α) = 0.35 (standard for small open economies)\n- Labor share (1-α) = 0.65\n- TFP (A) extracted via HP filter\n\n### Step 4: Investment Shock Impact\n\n**Shock Details**:\n- Total amount: 6.5 billion USD\n- Period: 2026-2033 (8 years)\n- Annual investment: 0.8125 billion USD\n\n**Impact Calculation**:\n1. **Base Scenario**: K_base grows at historical rate\n2. **Shock Scenario**: K_with = K_base + Investment\n3. **GDP Impact**: Y_star = Z * K_with^α\n\n**Key Formulas**:\n```\nK_with = K_base + Investment_t\nY_star_base = EXP(LnZ_trend + α*Ln(K_base))\nY_star_with = EXP(LnZ_trend + α*Ln(K_with))\nDifference = Y_star_with - Y_star_base\n% Change = Difference / Y_star_base\n```\n\n## How to Use the Excel File\n\n### Running the HP Filter:\n1. Open the **Production** sheet\n2. Go to **Data** tab → **Solver**\n3. Set **Objective**: $L$6 (Minimize)\n4. **By Changing Variable Cells**: $I$6:$I$27\n5. Click **Solve**\n6. Solver will optimize the LnZ_HP values to get a smooth trend\n\n### Viewing Results:\n1. **Investment Sheet** shows the GDP impact projection\n2. **Columns G and H** show absolute and percentage differences\n3. **Years 2026-2033** show the investment period impact\n4. **Years 2034-2041** show the sustained impact after investment ends\n\n### Extending Projections:\n- The Excel file extends projections through 2041\n- GDP projections beyond 2027 use the 2027 growth rate (5.0%)\n- Capital stock grows at the depreciation-adjusted rate after 2023\n- Investment shock impacts are incorporated into the capital stock\n\n## Key Results\n\nThe analysis will show:\n1. **Immediate Impact**: GDP increase during investment period (2026-2033)\n2. **Sustained Impact**: Higher GDP levels post-investment due to increased capital stock\n3. **Growth Rate**: Potential acceleration in growth rate during shock period\n4. **Long-term**: Persistent level shift in GDP trajectory\n\n## Assumptions and Limitations\n\n1. **Constant α**: Capital share remains at 0.35 throughout the projection period\n2. **No Crowding Out**: Investment adds to rather than replaces existing capital\n3. **Closed Economy**: No international capital flows or remittances considered\n4. **No Labor Growth**: Assumes constant labor input (or absorbed in TFP)\n5. **No Policy Changes**: Assumes no major structural reforms or policy shifts\n6. **Exchange Rate**: USD conversion assumes stable exchange rate\n7. **Constant TFP**: TFP trend extracted via HP filter remains stable\n\n## File Locations\n\n- **Excel File**: `/root/test-supply.xlsx`\n- **Source Code**: `/root/setup_excel.py`, `/root/enhance_excel.py`\n- **Documentation**: This file\n\n## Next Steps\n\n1. **Run Solver** to complete the HP filter optimization\n2. **Verify Results** in the Investment sheet\n3. **Sensitivity Analysis**: Test different α values (0.30, 0.40)\n4. **Alternative Scenarios**: Different investment timing or amounts\n5. **Robustness Checks**: Monte Carlo simulations for TFP uncertainty\n\n---\n\n**Created**: January 2026\n**Author**: Claude Code Analysis\n**Contact**: For questions or issues, refer to the source code and documentation
